# Agents · V · part 5

955 entries, verifier-prompt to VICTORIA_LT30_QUEST_EDGE_CASE_REVIEW. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **verifier-prompt**: Canonical definition of the **verifier** role in the forge multi-agent system. Mirrored into `references/workflows/researched-build.js` (Verify phase) — edit both together. Runs ONLY when the change renders UI (presence of UI, not tier). ([Tessellate-Studio/forge](https://github.com/Tessellate-Studio/forge/blob/HEAD/references/agents/verifier-prompt.md))
- **verifier-reachability**: Evaluates whether candidate sinks are authentically reachable from untrusted external sources. ([arcobaleno64/three-lens](https://github.com/arcobaleno64/three-lens/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-reachability.md))
- **verifier-reviewer**: Plan-critic Verifier persona. Decides whether an implementation plan shows the author actually read the code - symbol specificity, cross-check against the Grounding Brief, hidden assumptions. Spawn only inside a plan-critic workflow, with the full plan text and the Grounding Brief. ([smykla-skalski/sai](https://github.com/smykla-skalski/sai/blob/HEAD/plugins/plan-critic/agents/verifier-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **verifier-reviewer**: 檢驗者（第二階段）。實作完成後執行，負責跑隱藏測試、審查程式碼、 抓出各種作弊/取巧模式，並產出正式驗收報告。與 implementer 沒有共用上下文。 ([oilotaku/harness-template](https://github.com/oilotaku/harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-reviewer.md))
- **verifier-reviewer**: Skeptical reviewer for Oversight's core logic. Use at review checkpoints (after changing claims.py, checks.py, or verdict.py) to adversarially hunt for false-positive risk (blocking honest agents) and false-negative risk (believing false claims). ([sabarishraja/Claude-oversight](https://github.com/sabarishraja/Claude-oversight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-reviewer.md))
- **verifier-runner**: Runs tests, linters, type checks, build commands and returns only a concise pass/fail summary. USE PROACTIVELY when verification is needed; isolates noisy test output from the main conversation context. Subagent — pairs with main session for high-noise output isolation per HN consensus / DSSP §10.3 V verifier delegation. ([HarimxChoi/claude-setup](https://github.com/HarimxChoi/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/plugins/harim-base/agents/verifier-runner.md))
- **verifier-seam**: Reads everything that can reach a task-pipeline node's change (callers, callees, implementors, shared state, config and the neighbours' tests) and reports whether a contract moved under something that depends on it. Returns an eight-key tier report for graph.py certify. Use as the seam tier, a blind reading, when a node claims to be finished. Not for re-reading the diff, which the unit tier reads, or documentation, which the product tier reads. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-seam.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-security**: 檢驗者（選用第三階段）。針對牽涉到憑證、外部連線、使用者輸入處理、 權限控制的任務，額外做安全性驗收，避免注入、越權、機密外洩等問題。 ([oilotaku/harness-template](https://github.com/oilotaku/harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-security.md))
- **verifier-skeptic**: Adversarial verifier. Tries to REFUTE a finding's claimed impact; a claim survives only if a PoC reproduces real harm. ([xdisform/Spider](https://github.com/xdisform/Spider/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-skeptic.md) · ★23)
- **verifier-source-auditor**: Verifies citation traceability, link availability, and claim-source consistency. ([gqy20/IssueLab](https://github.com/gqy20/IssueLab/blob/HEAD/agents/rojasraleena-svg/.claude/agents/verifier-source-auditor.md) · ★12)
- **verifier-source-auditor**: Verifies source accessibility and claim-source consistency. ([gqy20/IssueLab](https://github.com/gqy20/IssueLab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-source-auditor.md) · ★12)
- **verifier-specialist**: Verify a flag candidate before accepting it. Use after a category specialist returns a solution — catches hallucinated flags, test/example strings, and decoy patterns that match the regex but aren't the real flag. ([iamkorun/hydra](https://github.com/iamkorun/hydra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-specialist.md))
- **verifier-standard**: Reviewer on the standard tier (sonnet, high effort). Use for the batch review of fast-lane commits when a wave or PR closes, and for prose-only or tooling-only changes. Never for a full-lane package that changes product behaviour. ([saeedamiri/agent-skills](https://github.com/saeedamiri/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/verifier-standard.md))
- **verifier-std**: The default verification agent. Executes a probe plan end to end and reports evidence per acceptance criterion. Pair it with verifier-deep, which designs the probes; drop to verifier-light for a single mechanical check. ([santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit](https://github.com/santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-std.md) · ★3)
- **verifier-strong**: Independent verifier for a package cycle on the strong tier (opus, high effort). Use for every full-lane verification - anything that changes what a user, a model or an API consumer sees. The brief names the role skill, the commit and the push authority. ([saeedamiri/agent-skills](https://github.com/saeedamiri/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/verifier-strong.md))
- **verifier-test-writer**: 檢驗者（第一階段）。在任何實作者開始寫程式之前，先依 task-spec 制定驗收測試，包含公開測試與「不給實作者看」的隱藏測試，用來防止作弊。 ([oilotaku/harness-template](https://github.com/oilotaku/harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-test-writer.md))
- **verifier-unit**: Reads only the code a task-pipeline node changed (the functions, classes and branches in the diff), runs the node's own check, and reports whether the change does what was asked without breaking its own unit. Returns an eight-key tier report for graph.py certify. Use as the unit tier, the closest blind reading, when a node claims to be finished. Not for style review, and not for callers, which the seam tier reads. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-unit.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-vera**: Checks, finding by finding, that a plan squad round's fixes resolved what was confirmed, judging the artifacts and not the fix note. Changes no artifact. Use for /speckit-squad-verify steps. ([Ocelle-HQ/ocelle-components](https://github.com/Ocelle-HQ/ocelle-components/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-vera.md))
- **verifier-visual**: Reads the contact sheet, the director record, the project linter's output and the rubric for a task-pipeline node, and reports whether what a user sees carries the intent the record set, state by state, with no gate item failing under a pass. Returns an eight-key tier report for graph.py certify. Use as the visual tier when a node on a flagship or product surface claims to be finished. Not for code, call graphs or documentation. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-visual.md) · ★7)
- **verifier-worker**: Re-verifies Waymark completion evidence before the main session prints WAYMARK_RUN_COMPLETE. ([Y4tacker/Waymark](https://github.com/Y4tacker/Waymark/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-worker.md) · ★20)
- **verifier-writer**: Writes ONE verifier from a scout's report — References/scripts/verify_&lt;name&gt;.mjs that recomputes a function's output from probed inputs bit for bit on both builds — captures for it, runs mutation, registers it in run_all. Returns verdict lines, mutation score and the manifest entries. Never edits facts/, other verifiers or the model. ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-writer.md) · ★6)
- **verifier.agent**: Runs every gate and checks the evidence record line by line against the definition of done. ([prabinpebam/focus-notes](https://github.com/prabinpebam/focus-notes/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/verifier.agent.md) · ★1)
- **verifier.agent**: **Name**: verifier **Role**: QA Engineer e Quality Gate Keeper **Status**: active **Model Tier**: standard ([B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise/blob/HEAD/.squad/agents/verifier.agent.md))
- **verifier.agent**: Final mechanical gate before a phase is declared done.
